# Chapter 1 General Provisions, Philosophy, and Scope

> **Position of this chapter**: The opening chapter of the Japanese canonical edition of the Final Requirements Specification (intent step5). It establishes the foundation that all individual requirements in Chapter 2 onward follow. The canonical articulation of the philosophy itself is `docs/planning/ver3/ver3-要件定義抽出書-v2.md §1` (hereafter "Extraction Document §1"); §1.2 of this chapter is merely a summary of it and a set of reference conventions.
> **Confirmation status**: The 5 articles of Extraction Document §1 were approved by the user on 2026-07-10 (as a set with `ver3-要望理解書-v1.md`). This chapter generalizes that approval into general provisions.

---

## 1.1 Purpose and Scope of Application

### 1.1.1 Purpose

This document finalizes the requirements for ver3 — **the next-generation civilization OS redesigned from zero**. Data, philosophy, and requirements are inherited from ver1/ver2, while the code is rebuilt from a zero base (a work decision from `ver3-要件定義抽出書-v2.md §5 DIFF-C-05`). The primary input for requirements is intent (the user's final instructions) and extraction from all past materials; the extraction results reside in Extraction Document v2, and the philosophy resides in Extraction Document §1. This chapter binds these together as the general provisions of the requirements specification.

### 1.1.2 Naming Conventions

| Name | Refers to | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| **ver3** | The next-generation system, redesigned from zero, defined by this document | Within this document, "ver3" always refers to this |
| **ver3-live** | The current production release (`it-hercules.uk` / `api.it-hercules.uk`) | The name used in this document for what existing repository documents have called "ver3 = current production." Distinct from ver3 |
| **ver1** | civilization-os (the original source of ideation and philosophy) | Reference material. Not subject to editing |
| **ver2** | The 01-要件 group, RTM, DESIGN, etc. of the current `it-hercules-laboratory` | The primary source from which requirements and philosophy are inherited |

- The naming conflict (the discrepancy with existing documents' usage of "ver3 = current production") has already been ruled on in Extraction Document `§4-1`, recorded in `§5 DIFF-C-01`. This document treats the table above as the sole authority.

### 1.1.3 Scope of Application

- The scope of application covers the entire process of ver3's design, implementation, operation, and governance.
- The workspace is this repository (`it-hercules-laboratory`) only. The `frontend/` and `backend/` of civilization-os are out of scope for changes.
- Where this document conflicts with individual requirements or design documents, resolution follows this order: **the invariant articles in 1.2 take highest priority**, then the general provisions of this document, then individual requirements.

---

## 1.2 Invariant Articles

Extraction Document §1 articulates the "foundation that does not change even as requirements change," which underlies all requirements, as **5 articles + 7 additional philosophies**. Individual requirements are replaced as specifications change, but this foundation remains unchanged, and all requirements ultimately trace back to one of these articles.

**Priority convention**: The design constraints mechanically imposed by each article in this section **take priority over all requirements in this document (Chapter 2 onward)**. Where an individual requirement conflicts with an article, either the requirement is revised, or it is permitted only as an explicitly stated scoped exception (such as the idempotent merge of series.parquet in ③). The wording, granularity, and consolidation of the articles are governed by Extraction Document §1 as canonical, and revisions there are automatically reflected in this section.

### 1.2.1 The 5 Articles (Summary)

| # | Article | In a word | Key design constraints (excerpt) |
|---|---|---|---|
| ① | Minimize the 10-year running cost | Run it at a cost one person can pay for 10 years | Do not make a resident DB the SSOT · recompute derived values on demand · LLM/Vision/FAISS default OFF · issue IDs/indexes only at the moment they are used · no low-latency requirement |
| ② | A fork culture where every user can improve | Publish it and let others fix it | Canonical source consolidated on GitHub · full source published as a single clone · improvement happens per Component via fork + lineage · a small fixed skeleton + fork extensions · no copyright claims, rewarded via contribution points instead |
| ③ | Truth is append-only | Never delete, never overwrite | R2 is INSERT ONLY (UPDATE/DELETE forbidden) · corrections are new records/snapshots · re-putting the same key returns 409 · state is an event sequence · PII is logically invalidated via an unused flag |
| ④ | Human-gate culture | Humans rule on the irreversible, the public, and the monetary | Human gates for production R2 writes, terms-of-service publication, actual GMO deposits, and classification finalization · no implementation until the 4 design gates are confirmed · sandbox improvements do not propagate to production until an explicit Promote |
| ⑤ | Nothing unverified is delivered | Passing the critic gate is a condition of delivery | Passing an independent critic (correctness/regression/design consistency) is mandatory · backed by adversarial verification · full pass of the V-model 5-point gate · a machine GATE PASS takes priority over a human declaration · RTM 100% / zero orphans in reverse RTM |

- The original evidence, full design constraints, and key linked requirements for each article are canonically referenced in the corresponding section of Extraction Document §1.

### 1.2.2 The 7 Additional Philosophies (Summary)

Philosophies discovered around the periphery of the 5 articles. **A sits above the 5 articles (the purpose = WHY)**; the other 6 are independent values that serve the 5 articles.

| Symbol | Philosophy | Relationship to the 5 articles |
|---|---|---|
| A | Observation civilization (observation is the central input) | **Sits above the 5 articles = the purpose**. The 5 articles are the HOW; A is what it is all run for |
| B | Natural selection (the good survives, the unneeded disappears) | The counterpart substrate of ②. Contends with ④'s human gate (tension #3) |
| C | Transparency supremacy (never conflate, show provenance, show cost too) | The intersection of ③ and ⑤. Public value_origin/provenance/`/costs` |
| D | Zero exaggeration, only what actually works | The user-facing counterpart of ⑤. No "incomplete" labels shown in the UI; stubs are stated explicitly |
| E | World thin, Culture thick | The structural premise of ②. Fixed World→…→SubComponent hierarchy · URLs use the Kernel UUID |
| F | Separation of persona | Karma / contribution / market reputation are not merged (ADR-H-08) |
| G | Permission-based input | The implementation intersection of ③ and ④. Permission edges only · invalid transitions return 409 · PATCH only permitted fields |

### 1.2.3 Tensions Within the Philosophy

The 6 sets of philosophies that mechanically conflict with each other (automation vs. human gate / distributed fork vs. central Promote / ruling authority / Truth immutability vs. legal deletion / verification gate vs. velocity / minimal storage vs. search UX) are catalogued in Extraction Document §1, "Tensions Within the Philosophy (6 sets)." This document adopts the current direction of resolution (unused flags, two-stage sandbox, separation of ruling authority, ver1 perfection, etc.) as the default.

---

## 1.3 The Three Scope Layers

ver3's requirements number 711 items (Extraction Document §2). They are divided into 3 layers + out-of-scope by implementation wave. The importance of a requirement is ranked S/A/B/C by Tier (Desire Strength (DS) × business value × dependency).

### 1.3.1 Overall Counts

| Wave | Count | Tier breakdown (S/A/B/C) | Definition |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Wave 1 (Core)** | 339 | 35 / 149 / 125 / 30 | The skeleton of the civilization OS. The minimal set to get running first |
| **Wave 2** | 216 | 1 / 11 / 193 / 11 | The main body that fell through in ver1/ver2 due to insufficient technology. ver3's reason for existing |
| **Experimental slot** | 21 | 0 / 3 / 7 / 11 | Opt-in with human ruling. Awaiting data accumulation / method replacement |
| Out of scope | 135 | 0 / 0 / 0 / 135 | Not implemented in the current wave (withdrawn, pending, or low priority) |
| **Total** | **711** | **36 / 163 / 325 / 187** | The Tier S36 newly surfaced items include video, overnight operation, GMO, etc. |

### 1.3.2 Representative Requirements per Wave

| Wave | Representative requirements (ID / Tier / short name) |
|---|---|
| Wave 1 | V3-OBS-01 (S) Generalize observation to 5 domains / V3-FND-01 (S) R2 INSERT ONLY / V3-AUT-01 (S) Magic-link-only authentication / V3-MKT-01 (S) Multi-channel P2P market / V3-OBS-22 (S) Observation core MVP scope / V3-AIP-31 (S) Human gate mandatory |
| Wave 2 | V3-VID-01 (S) Video generation pipeline (script → … → thumbnail) / natural-language-to-ScreenDef generation / 6-stage AI review, viewpoint gap detection / era snapshot restoration + culture dashboard / KPI feedback loop, civilization research newspaper / whole-OS fork |
| Experimental slot | Twin dual-entity broadcasting (method-replaced version, related to R-3) / cultural cycle prediction AI (awaiting data accumulation) / revival scope of medal-economy scarcity design (inflation prohibited, 90% attrition) |

- Wave assignment is based on desire strength and dependencies. Wave 1 adopts the policy of "perfecting one feature at a time, then expanding at the same quality bar" (the resolution of tension #5), and does not promote all features at once.

---

## 1.4 Glossary

| Term | Definition |
|---|---|
| **World** | The topmost world unit. The apex of the fixed hierarchy World→FeatureNode→Kernel→Component→SubComponent (philosophy E) |
| **Kernel** | The skeletal unit of a function. URLs are routed to the Kernel UUID, not the screen. Fixed in small number + extended via fork |
| **Component (civilization atom)** | The minimal unit of improvement/fork. Lineage (parent component_id, generation, version) is recorded in R2 |
| **SubComponent** | A subordinate unit that composes a Component. The leaf of the fixed hierarchy |
| **ScreenDef** | The canonical definition of screen structure as data (UI-as-data). JSON of navigation/screen structure |
| **ThemePack** | The canonical source of design. Propagates `--civ-*` tokens/design_tokens in bulk (the visual aspect of philosophy E) |
| **Truth** | The SSOT for civilization history, accounting, and state transitions. Operated INSERT ONLY on R2 (Article ③) |
| **R2** | Cloudflare R2. The sole persistent canonical layer (raw/normalized/derived/manifests/runs/logs/tags). A resident DB is not made the SSOT |
| **C-USB (Civilization-USB)** | The minimal contract unit for the system's synchronization, connection, and management. The input/output contract that makes Components interchangeable |
| **DS score** | Desire Strength. Requirement importance = DS × business value × dependency |
| **Tier / Wave** | Tier is the S/A/B/C importance rank. Wave is the implementation order (Wave 1/Wave 2/Experimental slot/Out of scope) |
| **lineage** | Genealogical metadata (uuid/parent_uuid/ancestor, etc.). Attached to every data structure capable of evolving |
| **value_origin** | The provenance of a measured value (direct observation/image-derived/estimated/interpolated). A tag that forbids conflation (philosophy C, Article ③) |
| **provenance / run_id** | Reproducibility metadata attached to derived artifacts (run_id/schema_version/input_hash/model_version) |
| **Personal Sandbox Realm** | A copy of production distributed to every authenticated user. A field for improvement experiments. Zero blast radius (Articles ②④) |
| **Promote** | The human gate that raises a sandbox improvement to production (Gate → optional vote → Admin Merge). Does not propagate automatically |
| **Human gate** | A checkpoint at which humans rule on the irreversible, the public, the monetary, and classification finalization (Article ④) |
| **Critic gate** | The delivery condition of passing an implementation through an independent critic (correctness/regression/design consistency) (Article ⑤) |
| **V-model 5-point gate** | The gate through which requirements, detailed design, transitions, UI, and test design must all pass before implementation. Test design is not exempted even with human Go |
| **RTM** | Requirement Traceability Matrix. 100% FR/NFR traced 1:1 via req_id↔test_case_id; zero orphaned tests in reverse RTM |
| **Karma / contribution / market reputation** | Trust / activity volume / peer evaluation. Kept on separate axes, not mixed (philosophy F, ADR-H-08) |

---

## 1.5 Change Management

### 1.5.1 Change Flow (via CR)

Requirement changes after freeze are not reflected unilaterally by AI, but processed **via a Change Request (CR)**.

```
CR filed → human confirmation → adoption decided → FR numbered → RTM reflected → implementation
```

- No implementation code is written until the 4-point design gate (requirements, detailed design, transitions, UI) is confirmed by a human (Article ④).
- The 5th gate, test design, is not exempted even by HUMAN-IMPL-SIGNOFF (Article ⑤).
- Adding retrofit tests is permitted after the gate has passed (`.cursor/rules/ihl-waterfall-v-model-gate.mdc`).

### 1.5.2 Freeze Conventions

| Subject | Meaning of freeze |
|---|---|
| 1.2 Invariant articles | Already approved in Extraction Document §1. Revisions are governed canonically by revisions to Extraction Document §1, requiring agreement at a level higher than a CR |
| schema/manifest/ID/input-output contracts | Frozen as a small fixed skeleton. Feature additions are done via fork (Article ②) |
| Individual requirements (FR/NFR) | Frozen wave by wave; changes go via CR. Unauthorized reflection after freeze is prohibited |

### 1.5.3 Withdrawal Ledger (R-1 through R-9)

Items the user has personally negated are not revived without authorization. The following is the withdrawal ledger from `ver3-要望理解書-v1.md §4`. In ver3, the treatment in the table below is fixed.

| # | What was withdrawn | Treatment in ver3 |
|---|---|---|
| R-1 | Automatic propagation of Semantics and automatic Script reactions in the 4D Viewer (exaggerated staging) | Not revived. "Zero exaggeration, only what actually works" is elevated to a design principle (philosophy D) |
| R-2 | The "autonomous, automatic, infinite evolution" portion of the Evolution Engine | Same as above (the exaggerated portion is entirely removed) |
| R-3 | The automatic-dialogue **method** of the twin dual entity | Only the method is withdrawn. The underlying desire for dual-entity broadcasting is ruled on in the experimental slot |
| R-4 | Gacha/NFT-ization of research points | Not revived |
| R-5 | The standalone "indulgence shop" UI and name | Maintains unification under the name "platinum coin shop" |
| R-6 | Cron jobs that fetch external information without the user's knowledge while absent | Consent-based; only active or explicitly permitted cron jobs |
| R-7 | The "the core stays permanently private" policy | Full publication (GitHub OSS) is adopted (Article ②) |
| R-8 | Exaggerated effect figures at the "70x" level for graphify and the like | Effects are measured empirically (measured at 2-3x) |
| R-9 | Integration of Builder's 15 editing modes into an IDE | Narrowing is maintained. Feature development is left to GitHub (a design-aesthetics ruling) |

- Aside from R-3 and R-9, the "dropped features" (video pipeline, memory OS, era engine, medal economy, component civilization, OS fork, BPCMS) have no evidence of withdrawal and are incorporated into the body of ver3 as Wave 2/experimental slot items.

# Chapter 2 — Wave 1 Requirements (Core)

> This chapter is the core of the ver3 final requirements definition (intent step5). It records, in full, **all 334 Wave-1 items with req_status = confirmed/confirmed(revised)**, organized by category, with "ID / Requirement statement (confirmed) / score_v2 / Acceptance viewpoint / Source trust level" for every item. ver3 is the next generation that redesigns the code from zero, while data, philosophy, and requirements are inherited from the current production system (ver3-live) (DIFF-C-05).
> Note that the **canonical source for rows with kind=constraint/non-functional appearing in this chapter's tables is Chapter 5**; their appearance here is a reference-only reprint showing Wave-1 scope.

- **Requirement statement (confirmed)**: Transcribed verbatim, without modification, from registry-v2's `statement` (the confirmed text reflecting the ruling).
- **score_v2**: Secondary scoring (composite of BV/UI/Dep/Diff). Sorted in descending order, indicating degree of coreness.
- **Acceptance viewpoint**: Pass conditions mechanically derived from the main clause of the requirement statement (no additional prose interpretation is added; only numbers, HTTP codes, and units present in `statement` are extracted as "verification keys").
- **Source trust level**: registry-v2's `trust` (High = primary design document/multiple rulings, Medium = derived/single source, Low = requires reconfirmation).

| Symbol | Meaning |
|---|---|
| Confirmed | Requirement adopted by ruling and assigned to Wave 1 |
| Confirmed (revised) | Requirement adopted with accompanying wording revision |

## 2.01 Observation (43 items)

**Design policy** — Observation is positioned as the central Input of the Civilization OS (the primary data for all functions), with targets branching into 5 domains: biological/artifact/digital/environmental/custom. Records are append-only, provenance (value_origin) is made explicit, and confirmation of species/subspecies identification is always a human gate; IoT integration, image annotation, and similarity search favor a determinism-first ladder and on-device computation to keep variable costs down.

| ID | Requirement statement (confirmed) | score_v2 | Acceptance viewpoint (derived from requirement main clause) | Source trust level |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| V3-OBS-01 | Observation is not insect-only; targets branch into 5 domains — biological/artifact/digital/environmental/custom (ADR-H-16) — and the first branch is used in the navigator. Non-biological targets have no taxonomy and use a tag-based approach; the biological data node is a generic EntityDataNode that handles arbitrary targets with the same structure. | 100 | Observation is not insect-only; targets branch into 5 domains — biological/artifact/digital/environmental/custom (ADR-H-16) — and the first branch is used in the navigator | High |

The OS never forces, deletes, or fixes items — it remains "a convenience feature that makes things easier." Unregistered items are detected via item_hash and trigger an additional confirmation prompt (yes/just this time/always add). | 100 | Measurement templates let users freely add items (numeric/text/select/image annotation/API/computed items) of their own choosing, held separately by sex, instar (1st instar/2nd instar/3rd instar early-late), and placement, forkable and subject to natural selection via platinum voting | High |

## 2.02 Individuals/Breeding (14 items)

**Design policy** — Individual Detail (A2) is treated as the "individual's home screen," consolidating parents (sire/dam), observation history, and pedigree statistics on a single screen. Renaming/promotion is never UPDATEd but appended as events (INSERT ONLY) to reproduce the name-at-the-time; ranking-style competitive hype elements are excluded in favor of presenting only statistics, trends, and transparency.

| ID | Requirement statement (confirmed) | score_v2 | Acceptance viewpoint (derived from requirement main clause) | Source trust level |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| V3-IND-01 | An observation session can be linked to a parent individual, recording sire and dam IDs; parents can be set either from observation input or from the individual detail screen, and the individual detail screen can navigate to the observation-history list (subject_ref canonical source is @individual/{individual_id}, OBS-IND-01–05). Parent-child (pedigree) linkage is the MVP's top priority — the starting point of the pedigree flow and research data. The family tree reconstructs a lineage tree from any individual via buildPedigree. | 100 | An observation session can be linked to a parent individual, recording sire and dam IDs; parents can be set either from observation input or from the individual detail screen, and the individual detail screen can navigate to the observation-history list (subject_ref canonical source is @individual/{individual_id}, OBS-IND-01–05) | High |

Birth → observation → schedule → transfer → death → specimen → marketplace are displayed chronologically on a single-screen timeline, also integrating ownership history, species/morphology, biological card, statistics, and environment (time series). | 64 | Individual Detail (A2) is treated as the "individual's home screen," consolidating six cultures onto a single screen: observation (latest observation/history/growth-curve graph), schedule (auto-generated), observation templates, DataSource (shelf/thermometer/light meter), marketplace (offers), and improvement culture | Medium |

## 2.03 Papers/Research (15 items)

**Design policy** — Papers are held as structured data (Living Paper), matching condition P against user observations field-by-field (Paper Match). Reproducibility metadata (run_id/model/input_hash) is attached, visualizing research gaps in the field, with prose treated as a later-applied aesthetic layer that lowers the barrier to writing.

## 2.04 Wiki/Knowledge (17 items)

**Design policy** — Layer a persistent sub-brain (a cross-cutting knowledge layer) of distilled knowledge on top of the information sources (bulletin boards/papers/observations). A deterministic search ladder concentrates model calls into a single distillation pass, enforcing mandatory citations and index synchronization; a two-layer boundary keeps only stable knowledge in the wiki layer and high-frequency data in the Truth layer, eliminating dependence on any one individual.

A daily batch ingests bulletin-board/paper content into the system Wiki, building a large Wiki atop per-board summaries/aggregates in a hierarchical structure that raises accuracy while lowering cost. | 75 | An agent-maintained persistent Wiki (sub-brain) layered on top of information sources (bulletin boards/papers/observations) — a cross-cutting knowledge layer (the karpathy LLM Wiki pattern) that, unlike RAG's re-search-every-time approach, accumulates and continuously updates knowledge distilled once | High |

## 2.05 Marketplace/Economy (27 items)

**Design policy** — Operate a P2P marketplace and a three-tier economy (commercial 3% / trade 8% / fork 10%) that is complete using R2 (JSON) alone. Listing is a state machine, the ledger is double-entry bookkeeping, and all logs are append-only, closing off illegitimate transitions; rather than eliminating bad actors, incentive design that lets honest trading pay off drives natural selection.

| ID | Requirement statement (confirmed) | score_v2 | Acceptance viewpoint (derived from requirement main clause) | Source trust level |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| V3-MKT-10 | Upon trade completion, book 8% of sale proceeds as a "system-maintenance-fee tax" liability against the seller; if paid within a 30-day grace period from the point of occurrence, the same amount is converted 1:1 into capital-contribution credit; unpaid amounts past the grace period are marked fee_unpaid, incurring a monthly Fibonacci Δcount (karma, #08) counted from the month the trade completed (fully clearing that trade's balance in a given month stops the Δcount originating from that trade; it does not exclude accumulation with other causes). Recovery does not target full collection via an arbitrary bank-transfer method. | 125 | Upon trade completion, book 8% of sale proceeds as a "system-maintenance-fee tax" liability against the seller; if paid within a 30-day grace period from the point of occurrence, the same amount is converted 1:1 into capital-contribution credit; unpaid amounts past the grace period are marked fee_unpaid, incurring a monthly Fibonacci Δcount (karma, #08) counted from the month the trade completed (fully clearing that trade's balance in a given month stops the Δcount originating from that trade; it does not exclude accumulation with other causes). Verification key: 8%, 30 days | High |

## 2.06 Karma/Contribution (20 items)

**Design policy** — Karma is a two-layer independent model (a governance/economic penalty layer) of "karma value ([-100, +100])" and "karma count." There is no active increase — only time-based recovery — and repeat offenses are escalated via a Fibonacci progression; contribution is tracked independently across the 3 axes of research/capital/development without merging them, and grant conditions are managed in a unified, no-code-editable format.

Numeric values for disputes/trades never modify the value directly — they are treated as Δcount. ## 2.07 Bulletin Board/Community (9 items)

**Design policy** — "The Plaza of Knowledge (/knowledge)" is the integrated hub, with 3 bulletin-board types (explanation/gripe/improvement) plus a fork-improvement cycle that lets the civilization self-repair. Posts are append-only (corrections are appended), the poster's name is fixed to the confirmed username, and a Universal Cite mechanism lets every entity be referenced with a single click.

Since entry points, primary tasks, and Truth storage destinations differ, they are arranged in parallel as pillars rather than tabs; the hub screen is a single navigation layer, and duplicate listing across tabs and cards is prohibited. Reach each pillar's key operation from the home screen within 3 clicks. | 100 | Rename the bulletin board to "The Plaza of Knowledge (/knowledge)" as the integrated hub, bundling 3 pillars: (1) the official bulletin board (per-screen gripes/improvements), (2) papers, (3) the GitHub bulletin board (per-feature improvements/history). Each screen also carries component edit/swap/fork/Builder-create-publish, a list of related components, and an IDE entry point; the improvement-proposal/fork list displays in a popup with tag filtering, without navigating away from the screen. ## 2.08 Authentication/Users (21 items)

**Design policy** — Authentication is passwordless, using magic links sent to email only; password, OAuth, SMS, and social/SNS login are intentionally not supported. The authentication boundary is default-deny, adopting opaque sessions, immutable handles, and open registration; actual email sending is done via the VPS, and production SMTP keys are a human gate.

## 2.09 Translation/i18n (6 items)

**Design policy** — UGC original text alone shall be stored append-only to R2, and translation shall be executed on the viewer's device (client-side compute resources) according to the viewer's locale to keep costs down. UI text shall be keyed as {screen}.{component}.{field}, hardcoded Japanese text and missing text-key detection shall be caught by CI, and display language (locale) shall be a required selection at new registration.

| ID | Requirement text (final) | score_v2 | Acceptance viewpoint (derived from requirement main clause) | Source confidence |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| V3-I18-06 | UGC (board posts, two-person-room messages, listing descriptions, free-form text, etc.) shall store only the original text in the author's language append-only on R2, and translation shall be executed on the viewer's device (client-side compute resources) according to the viewer's locale. Server-side translation shall not be the first choice. Human-readable free text on new FeatureNodes shall inherit this UGC translation contract by default. | 80 | UGC (board posts, two-person-room messages, listing descriptions, free-form text, etc.) shall store only the original text in the author's language append-only on R2, and translation shall be executed on the viewer's device (client-side compute resources) according to the viewer's locale | High |
| V3-I18-08 | UI text resources shall be keyed ({screen}.{component}.{field}), with new hardcoded Japanese additions discouraged by implementation conventions; missing text keys shall be detected by CI and never shown as "unimplemented" text to the user. Unsupported locales shall use a fallback chain (country-specific → language-specific → en → key) without blank text, and translated text shall keep a DOM readable by screen readers. UI JSON itself shall not hardcode text but reference i18n JSON on R2 (minimal wiring with next-intl, etc.). | 64 | UI text resources shall be keyed ({screen}.{component}.{field}), with new hardcoded Japanese additions discouraged by implementation conventions; missing text keys shall be detected by CI and never shown as "unimplemented" text to the user | High |
| V3-I18-02 | Display language (locale) shall be a required selection at new registration/onboarding, and nationality/country code shall be unnecessary (optional or removed). onboardingComplete:true must never be set while locale remains unset, and Accept-Language shall only be used as a suggested default, never confirmed without consent. | 32 | Display language (locale) shall be a required selection at new registration/onboarding, and nationality/country code shall be unnecessary (optional or removed) | High |
| V3-I18-01 | The cross-cutting translation/i18n (#21) feature shall be connected via contract to authentication, UI, boards, courts, market, and karma, with UI language following the registered language setting. | 16 | The cross-cutting translation/i18n (#21) feature shall be connected via contract to authentication, UI, boards, courts, market, and karma, with UI language following the registered language setting | High |
| V3-I18-03 | The display language (locale) shall be changeable at any time from settings/profile, with the entire product UI switching immediately upon successful save (ideally without requiring re-login); locale shall be independent from country and shall not be blocked by karma suspension or dispute status. | 9 | The display language (locale) shall be changeable at any time from settings/profile, with the entire product UI switching immediately upon successful save (ideally without requiring re-login); locale shall be independent from country and shall not be blocked by karma suspension or dispute status | High |

## 2.10 UI/UX (25 items)

**Design policy** — Define the UI declaratively as data (ScreenDef/JSON) rather than code, converting it into a single React Renderer. The design source of truth is the ThemePack (--civ-* tokens, light/dark 2-pack mandatory); evolve the UI naturally through full componentization plus evaluation/karma, and apply the cognitive-load constitution (one purpose per screen, one primary CTA, within 3 clicks) to all screens.

| ID | Requirement text (final) | score_v2 | Acceptance viewpoint (derived from requirement main clause) | Source confidence |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| V3-UIX-08 | Adopt a REFRAME narrowing that limits the UI builder's responsibility to layout, visual design, and linking existing functionality. Functionality shall only be selected/linked from a catalog (existing Component/Kernel/hybrid_slot/registered API connectors); capabilities not in the catalog shall not be invented within the Builder. Development of new Components, new APIs, new routes, and pipelines shall happen outside the Builder (repo/Docker/CI/GitHub PR). | 80 | Adopt a REFRAME narrowing that limits the UI builder's responsibility to layout, visual design, and linking existing functionality | High |
| V3-UIX-16 | The design source of truth shall be the ThemePack (--civ-* tokens/design_token.yaml/UI primitive catalog); light/dark 2-pack mandatory, applying CSS variables across all screens via a world-common default + per-user overrides + fork lineage. A single change to the design template propagates uniformly across all screens at once. | 80 | The design source of truth shall be the ThemePack (--civ-* tokens/design_token.yaml/UI primitive catalog); light/dark 2-pack mandatory, applying CSS variables across all screens via a world-common default + per-user overrides + fork lineage | High |

Cover UI and functionality with OSS as much as possible (since it is refined and has fewer bugs); GPL/AGPL products shall be used for UI reference only, confirming license compatibility. | 60 | Adopt an OSS base for UI (Next.js 15 + shadcn/ui, a 5ch-style template for boards, tldraw/flexlayout-react, etc.), fully discarding the ugly and hard-to-use existing civ-os UI while reusing only the hooks/api layer | Medium |

## 2.11 Infrastructure/Platform (19 items)

**Design policy** — The sole persistent source of truth shall be Cloudflare R2, consistently enforcing INSERT ONLY (no-overwrite = re-put to the same key rejected with 409); resident databases shall be projections/caches only. World state shall flow one-way from Command → pure-function Reducer (Kernel) → storage, with C-USB as the minimum unit, lineage metadata + hash chains guaranteeing sameness of civilization identity, and heavy computation offloaded to the client device.

## 2.12 Cost/Operations (4 items)

**Design policy** — Top priority is a fixed-cost structure sustainable for 10 years even without user growth, avoiding usage-based billing (embedding APIs, etc.) and placing compute resources on the user side. Select the cheapest infrastructure (.uk / Sakura 512MB / a single Cloudflare account), and put in place nginx+certbot/pm2 operational procedures and a cost-transparency dashboard (/costs).

| ID | Requirement text (final) | score_v2 | Acceptance viewpoint (derived from requirement main clause) | Source confidence |
|---|---|---|---|---|

The operating-cost target shall be around 300–1000 yen per month, centered on serverless (Cloudflare Pages/Workers/R2) plus a minimal VPS (for mail/magic-link and webhook relay only), aiming for zero server cost, automatic scaling, no maintenance, and zero operational headcount ("a civilization run just by plugging in a USB"). Read paths (search, etc.) shall be accessible without an account for load distribution, while only write paths require authentication; maintenance processing shall be batched (every 5 minutes/1 hour/1 day). DDL constraints shall be ported into the kernel, running purely on R2 + POST. | 80 | Top priority shall be a cost structure sustainable for 10 years even without user growth, absolutely avoiding usage-based billing that scales with user count (variable costs such as embedding APIs), placing compute resources on the user side instead. ## 2.13 Security/Legal (25 items)

**Design policy** — Personal information shall be processed in the order PII detection → masking → storage, never retaining plaintext PII or addresses (identification via UUID/UserID/display name only). Secrets shall be injected at runtime without leaking, external ingestion shall be limited to permitted layers, user code shall run sandboxed and isolated, and logic shall be fixed in the API with the UI kept as a thin skin. Changes to legal wording are a human gate.

Truth/R2 shall never hold plaintext PII (name/address/phone/bank account); user identification shall use only user_id_hash, and email_index shall be keyed with a SHA256 hash. Collected observation PII shall not be stored in Truth in plaintext, but either encrypted or held only for the session. PII (name/email/phone/address/SNS ID/credentials/.env values/location) shall be detected across the document set and masked as {{PII:TYPE}}, generating a candidate list for the human gate, a clean dataset, and a diff report, while the original remains unmodified and isolated. | 100 | Personal information shall always be processed in the order PII detection → masking → storage before saving, absolutely prohibiting storage, restoration, or log retention of pre-mask data | High |

## 2.14 Courts/Governance (12 items)

**Design policy** — Developers and founders shall never act as judges; disputes are limited to 3 categories — market, boards, and bug fixes — processed via a two-person-room dialogue leading to agreement or a deadline. Platinum voting (1 vote = 1 PT) is triggered only upon publication, precedents accumulate append-only on R2 and remain citable, and the governance rule set and the OS itself evolve through forking and voting.

## 2.15 AI Operations/Development Process (69 items)

**Design policy** — Separate the implementation (EXEC) and audit (AUDIT) roles into distinct agents, requiring a human gate (human-in-the-loop) and prohibiting one-click full automation. Through model division of labor, the V-model 5-point gate, Spec-Driven design / Intent-Driven development (ISP), prioritize "machine GATE PASS over a human's completion declaration," accumulating results via overnight batch runs while humans only do OK/NG.

| ID | Requirement text (final) | score_v2 | Acceptance viewpoint (derived from requirement main clause) | Source confidence |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| V3-AIP-03 | Implemented artifacts shall always be delivered only after passing an independent critic/audit agent (the EXEC implementation agent and the AUDIT agent shall be separate agents). Rubber-stamping/checkbox theater is prohibited; PASS is granted only when accompanied by real browser verification, and a completion mark shall only be given when measured evidence exists. A feature that doesn't work shall never be described as "working." | 125 | Implemented artifacts shall always be delivered only after passing an independent critic/audit agent (the EXEC implementation agent and the AUDIT agent shall be separate agents) | High |
| V3-AIP-31 | A human gate/human-in-the-loop shall be mandatory: one-click full automation is prohibited; AI is an assistant, not a decision-maker (it presents candidates and the human chooses). At human-only gates such as GMO production, final legal wording, and Tier D physical keys, the machine shall never fake a [x]; it must always stop and report. Automated processing shall be opt-in; AI shall never auto-change culture, balances, or world law, and shall not auto-infer or auto-generate. AI's reasoning shall be disclosed, prohibiting black boxes, and uncertain points shall never be silently filled in but recorded as pending. | 125 | A human gate/human-in-the-loop shall be mandatory: one-click full automation is prohibited; AI is an assistant, not a decision-maker (it presents candidates and the human chooses) | High |

# Chapter 3 Second-Wave Requirements

## 3.0 Definition and positioning of the second wave

The second wave is **the core theme cluster to be started once the first wave (observation core / thought core / append-only foundation) is running green**. What gathers here are features that "fell through in ver1/ver2 due to insufficient technology, but whose underlying desire was never withdrawn and is still alive" — that is, the substance of the **desires v2 scoring weighted most heavily: 'wanted to build but couldn't build.'**

| Item | Content |
|---|---|
| Count (§2 machine assignment) | **216 items** (Confirmed 195 / Confirmed (Amended) 17 / Pending 4) |
| Meaning of the wave | Rides on top of the first-wave foundation. Cannot stand alone — presupposes observation → 1st–4th-order transformation, append-only R2, authentication, and the similarity-search ladder |
| Score distribution | Tier S band: 1 (V3-VID-01) / Tier A upper band is concentrated in video mass production, the shooting chamber, peer review, and citations (the DS4 resurgence band) |
| Granularity of this chapter | Rather than enumerating all 216 items, they are bundled into **6 clusters**, each attached with a "representative requirements table" and a "pre-start gate." The authoritative assignment of every item is the §2 artifact `wave-tables.md` |

**Second-wave common start conditions** (preconditions preceding all clusters):

| # | Condition | Basis |
|---|---|---|
| C-W2-1 | The first-wave observation ITO pipeline (IN→Transform→OUT) is measured green | V3-OBS-08 / V3-OBS-43 |
| C-W2-2 | R2/Truth's INSERT ONLY, lineage metadata, and snapshot foundation are operating | V3-FND-01 / V3-FND-15 |
| C-W2-3 | The deterministic similarity-search ladder (whitelist→subset→embedding) is operating | V3-OBS-10 / V3-OBS-11 |
| C-W2-4 | The immutable clauses (§1) — mandatory human gate, ban on one-click full automation — are already applied to all lines | V3-AIP-31 / V3-AIP-96 |

The 6 clusters below are shown in order of desire intensity.

---

## 3.1 Automated video mass-production line (second-wave core, strongest recurring desire)

**Positioning**: Among the 12 desire themes, this is the **oldest yet most recent, strongest, most recurring** (28 sources, 4 months, 5 channels). It is the desire to turn script→image→subtitle→voice→composite→thumbnail/metadata→posting into a single automated line, stockpile it in a posting stack, and make "post daily with one week's worth of work" a reality. It is taken up as the top priority immediately after the observation foundation.

**Design policy (irrevocable clause)**: "**A human hand at the split points leaves fingerprints. That's what becomes the soul**" (user's original wording) — the OK/NG at split points and the human gate on the publish button are not an efficiency compromise but essential value, and will not be removed even as future automation rates rise. It is treated as an **irrevocable clause** on par with "values > worldview > consistency" and "don't chase numbers."

### Representative requirements

| V3-VID-07 | 45 | 3 | Automatically dispatch daily 1-minute shorts to multiple PFs, drawing on dissatisfaction with the philosophy, development struggles, and opacity as material. Three streams — research/system/development-hardship — and let AI itself think up the generation method | Confirmed |

Redundant configuration with an external KV as primary and a self-hosted R2 hot standby; automatically swap in a more comprehensible video via voting (the original is retained) | Confirmed |
| V3-VID-STORE | 30 | 2 | Don't store the video body itself on R2/the system — place it externally (YouTube/TikTok/X) and retain only the routing table, URL, purpose, version, history, and linked thread | Confirmed |

## 3.2 Natural language → ScreenDef / UI-as-data generation

**Positioning**: The desire for "just say the screen name and purpose in natural language and the UI appears." The naive keyword parser that sank v1/v2 has been resolved via LLM structured output (builder.json feasible_2026). **The foundation itself — UI = data (ScreenDef) — is first wave** (V3-UIX-17/18); the generation assistant that rides on top is second wave.

### Representative requirements

| ID | score_v2 | DS | Requirement (summary) | Wave |
|---|--:|:--:|---|---|
| V3-UIX-17 | 48 | 3 | Declaratively define UI not as code but as data (ScreenDef/JSON), converted to a single React Renderer (adding a page = adding JSON). **A homemade plain HTML/JS renderer is not adopted; Next.js/React + ScreenDef is the implementation approach** | **First wave (foundation)** |
| V3-AIP-82 | 27 | 3 | AI is restricted to a role of generating World/UI JSON (AST) or Command[], never writing UI code/world directly; the Renderer converts it to shadcn/ui etc. **AI proposals are fixed to a top separate layer and added to the AST only once adopted by a human** (AI is not given delete/world-rewrite authority) | Second wave |
| V3-UIX-61 | 18 | 3 | Just say the screen name and purpose in natural language to generate UI JSON (+ ASCII wireframe); natural-language diff editing of existing UI (editUI) is also possible. **AI produces an 80% draft, the human fine-tunes the remaining 20% in the Builder**, with schema validation via Zod etc. before saving. The generation prompt itself is also forkable | Second wave |
| V3-UIX-09 | 27 | 3 | The UI builder is two-tier: Web edition = simplified version (fork/declarative operations), Docker side = IDE-like L1 component / L0 code editing. **Maintains the narrowing (R-9) that leaves feature development outside the Builder (on GitHub)** | Second wave |
| V3-UIX-36 | 27 | 3 | Comprehensively map screen transitions and visually walk through "what to click to get where" locally; AI-generate navigation.json; decide the standard specification for the back button | Second wave |
| V3-AIP-87 | 27 | 3 | Reverse-generate the current spec as JSON from the implementation, and reflect diffs via "adapt" (spec → diff → auto-apply → auto-test). Always take a full commit before applying, so rollback is possible | Second wave |

### Pre-start gates (ScreenDef generation)

| Gate | Content | Basis |
|---|---|---|
| **UI-as-data foundation first** | Presupposes that ScreenDef/Renderer (V3-UIX-17/18) is green in the first wave. The generator rides only on top of the foundation | V3-UIX-17 |
| **Schema-validation gate** | Generated UI JSON is schema-validated via Zod etc. before saving (the 20% of the 80/20 flow always passes through human verification) | V3-UIX-61 |
| **AI authority narrowing** | AI proposals occupy a separate layer, added to the AST only upon human adoption. AI is not given delete/world-rewrite authority | V3-AIP-82 |
| **REFRAME narrowing (R-9)** | The Builder is limited to layout, design, and linking of existing features. **Any expansion toward "developing features themselves via an integrated IDE" falls under withdrawal ledger R-9 and its revival is banned** (authoritative source: V3-UIX-08 REFRAME) | V3-UIX-08 / R-9 |

## 3.3 Standard shooting chamber / BPCMS color specification

**Positioning**: The observation desire to "capture mm precision, color, luminance, humidity, and temperature all at once from a single photo" (52 sources, 4 months, all channels). The observation core is partially implemented in the first wave, but **the standard-shooting-chamber physical unit, strict BPCMS, and per-part Lab feature values are second wave**, since they presuppose jig fabrication and an ADR unifying embedding dimensions.

### Representative requirements

| ID | score_v2 | DS | Requirement (summary) | Wave |
|---|--:|:--:|---|---|
| V3-OBS-12 | 48 | 4 | Hercules standard shooting chamber (40cm matte-gray box, CRI/Ra95+ 6500K fixed LED, ColorChecker Nano, 5mm grid/ArUco, motorized turntable, light-shielded/vibration-isolated). Have the subject climb a transparent polycarbonate rod and rotate the whole rod for low-stress RAW capture of 5 viewpoints in `.dng` (authoritative) + `.jpg`, with automatic upload | Second wave |
| V3-OBS-13 | 36 | 3 | Freeze-establish the beetle color-measurement standard specification **BPCMS v1.0**. **Store raw images uncorrected (compatible with OBS-23); BPCMS computes gray-card-corrected Lab as a post-processed "derived value layer."** Jig fabrication ordering is an R1 human gate; the CIE76→CIEDE2000 switch is confirmed separately | Second wave |
| V3-VID-12 | 16 | 1 | Nationwide participants capture same-quality images via smartphone (identical lighting/distance/background/color reference). Reproducibility prioritized over precision. HDR, night mode, AI correction, filters, and flash are banned | Second wave |
| V3-OBS-14 | 24 | 2 | Feed per-part average L\*a\*b\* (head/pronotal horn/prothorax/elytra) + variance + color histogram + DINOv2/CLIP/ViT embeddings into similarity-search rerank (color/size components); analysis logic is an independently forkable node per template | **First wave (supply side)** |
| V3-PPR-11 | 40 | 2 | Make R2's images and analysis data API-retrievable for research purposes (CC0 recommended), turning it into public infrastructure (open science) for color research | Second wave |

### Pre-start gates (shooting chamber / BPCMS)

| Gate | Content | Basis |
|---|---|---|
| **Embedding-dimension unification ADR (most critical)** | The observation pipeline's 384 dimensions and the shooting chamber's 768 dimensions are **left mismatched (DIFF-C-18)**. Before starting, finalize the dimension-unification ADR and consolidate into a single EmbeddingBackend Protocol (V3-OBS-09) requiring L2 normalization and banning NaN. **Do not proceed with shooting-chamber implementation without the dimension being fixed** | DIFF-C-18 / V3-OBS-09 |
| **Uncorrected raw image × derived Lab, two-layer** | Reconcile "photos should not be color-corrected, I want the raw truth" (the user) with BPCMS colorimetry through a two-layer approach: **store the raw image uncorrected, and compute derived Lab in post-processing from the embedded color reference** (compatible with OBS-23) | V3-OBS-13 / V3-OBS-23 |
| **Jig physical-fabrication gate (R1)** | Ordering physical-jig fabrication such as the shooting chamber is an **R1 human gate**, since it is an irreversible cost. Start from a scaled-down proposal that pre-secures reproducibility using a printable scale sheet + ArUco and smartphone ProRAW / fixed color temperature and exposure | V3-OBS-13 / observation.json |
| **Color-space version freeze** | Freeze the analysis engine via semantic versioning (v1.x = CIE76, v2.x = CIEDE2000), fixing Major per paper. The timing of the CIE76→CIEDE2000 switch is confirmed separately | V3-PPR-10 / V3-OBS-13 |

## 3.4 Era snapshot / cultural visualization (two layers: record → visualize)

**Positioning**: A rewindable world capable of "reproducing my civilization as of March 2026" (22 sources). The era engine is a trinity of (a) snapshot/rewind (b) cultural visualization dashboard (c) cyclical prediction, but **the solid winning line, (a)(b), is placed in the second wave**, while **the cold-start item (c) cyclical prediction is split off into Chapter 4 (experimental zone)**.

| V3-WIK-33 | 24 | 4 | Manage cultural state via two layers, "record (quarterly R2 snapshot) → visualize (trend dashboard)," with era comparison, partial copy, diff display, and cultural-sphere clustering. **The cyclical-prediction AI is an experimental zone of heuristics + human judgment until data accumulates** (→ §4.2) | Second wave (record/visualize) / experimental zone (prediction) |
| V3-UIX-58 | 18 | 3 | Visualize dialogue, generated output, evolution log, lineage, and world-data change via a 2D spatial map (nodes + edges) + timeline; color-code time travel and diffs between two versions via snapshotVersion history. **The 4D exaggerated staging (R-1) is not included** | Second wave |
| V3-BBS-33 | 4 | 2 | Visualize bulletin-board statistics (post trends, culture score, time-of-day heatmap, tag frequency, AI-generation rate); separate local statistics from whole-civilization statistics into different screens | Second wave |

| **Exclusion of 4D exaggerated staging (R-1)** | Of "3D × time = 4D stereoscopic playback," **the 'non-functional exaggeration' parts — Semantic auto-propagation, Script auto-reaction — were self-negated and withdrawn by the user as "that's a lie" (R-1)**. Visualization is limited to **the reality line only**: timeline scrubber + snapshot playback + 2D ForceGraph. Exaggerated staging is out of scope | R-1 / V3-IND-26 / V3-UIX-58 |

## 3.5 KPI feedback / improvement loop (Evaluator)

**Positioning**: A common loop that collects generated-content, bulletin-board, and video metrics and feeds them back into AI's next proposals. **Numbers (view counts) remain reference values only, with values > worldview > consistency prioritized above all** to structurally ban radicalization (the user's immutable discipline).

The LLM judge is a different model family from the proposer, and always passes through the deterministic check first | Second wave |
| V3-PPR-08 | 64 | 4 | Turn citations into an independent entity, building a bidirectional bulletin-board↔paper↔observation link per ProjectId. Even if the citation target is private/deleted, preserve the body via a tombstone mini-card without breaking it — Citations are never deleted (INSERT ONLY) | Second wave |
| V3-PPR-25 | 36 | 3 | Store the research-cycle layer (paper/review/hypothesis/replication/gap/comment/correction) append-only, treating a paper as the starting point of the next research. A submission is a correction submission, not an edit; tags are aggregated across history | Second wave |
| V3-AIP-75 | 27 | 3 | Evaluator common architecture (feedback.csv → embedding clustering → automatic decision/creation model update). Treats failures (NG reasons) as the most important training data | Second wave |
| V3-VID-16 | 12 | 2 | Incorporate video metrics into observation data and feed them back into AI's next proposals | Second wave |
| V3-AIP-67 | 24 | 4 | Periodically batch-collect accumulated GitHub Issues/bulletin-board posts (100 at a time), summarize into 3–5 lines with the LLM plus keyword extraction, and display as an AI-summary thread (INSERT ONLY, re-summarization is a new version) | **First wave (operating mechanism)** |

### Pre-start gates (KPI feedback)

| Gate | Content | Basis |
|---|---|---|
| **Anti-radicalization gate** | The evaluation priority order is "values > worldview > consistency > accuracy > numbers." **Numbers are reference values only; SoulProfile is referenced by all generation to structurally ban radicalization**. Design-stage blocking of any path where KPI feedback drifts toward "radicalization via view-count optimization" (SoulProfile reference links with §4 experimental zone V3-AIP-85) | V3-VID-15 / V3-AIP-85 |
| **Legal gate on unmanned acquisition** | Avoid mechanisms such as cron that fetch information on their own while the user is absent, since this carries legal risk under Japanese law; instead **adopt a method that hits the API only on the user's active operation of opening the Web app** (the same applies to metrics collection) | V3-SEC-52 |
| **Deterministic-first** | When using an LLM for review/evaluation, always insert a deterministic check (schema conformance, existing-test reproducibility) beforehand, keeping the LLM independent as a different model family from the proposer | V3-PPR-05 / V3-AIP-04 |

## 3.6 Overnight operation (the operating mechanism that carries the second-wave lines)

**Positioning**: The **operating mechanism that runs the mass-production/collection/distillation lines of §3.1–3.5 above "while asleep, stacking results in the morning review stack."** The operating mechanism itself (V3-AIP-96/78/67) belongs to the **first wave** as the backbone of dependencies, but since the **batch cargo it carries (video-script mass production, KPI aggregation) is second wave**, it is documented in this chapter as an operating convention.

### Representative requirements

| ID | score_v2 | DS | Requirement (summary) | Wave |
|---|--:|:--:|---|---|
| V3-AIP-96 | 100 | 5 | During hours the human is absent, use surplus Claude Code sessions/scheduled execution to auto-run overnight batches (**video-script generation**, wiki distillation, material metadata-ization, KPI aggregation/improvement proposals); **stack results in the morning review stack so the human only does OK/NG** (the operating mechanism is first wave; when second-wave deliverables such as video scripts are the cargo, they follow that cargo's own pre-start gate) | **First wave** |
| V3-AIP-78 | 27 | 3 | Divide large-volume tasks into overnight/weekly execution packs for AI (Auto surplus capacity) to maximize accuracy. **Fully unmanned one-click full automation is banned; the undecided 10% keeps a human gate** | **First wave** |
| V3-CST-03 | 32 | 4 | Three-tier model routing (exploration at the lowest tier, e.g. Haiku). Mandatory documentation of token budget, retry ceiling, and failure-escalation conditions in the delegation template; keep exploratory delegation to one tier, never spawning grandchild agents | Second wave |
| V3-AIP-89 | 32 | 2 | Complete separation of the K/B/F/A/C layers; only the connection interfaces (protocols) are fixed as JSON, with contents freely swappable (Lego-style), a structure that survives forking without breaking | Second wave |

### Pre-start gates (overnight operation)

| Gate | Content | Basis |
|---|---|---|
| **Maintain the human gate (irrevocable clause)** | One-click full automation is banned. Present overnight-batch results **stacked in the morning review stack in an OK/NG-only state**; the undecided 10% always keeps a human gate. Unmanned operation is impossible | V3-AIP-96 / V3-AIP-78 / V3-AIP-31 |
| **Prevent cost runaway** | Mandatory documentation of token budget, retry ceiling, and escalation conditions in the delegation template; grandchild agents are banned. Append one line of estimated token budget to each PLAN | V3-CST-03 |
| **Legal gate** | External acquisition at night follows the unmanned-acquisition gate of §3.5 (V3-SEC-52), avoiding crawling that does not originate from an active operation | V3-SEC-52 |

# Chapter 4 Experimental Track

## 4.0 Definition and Operating Frame of the Experimental Track

The experimental track is a layer that holds, under an **opt-in (default off), human-ruling-required** framework, themes where **the underlying desire is still alive but the implementation "method" has been withdrawn — or where a cold start due to insufficient data would occur**. Rather than mixing these into the second-wave core, they are kept as isolated experimental modules, and the person himself decides whether and in what form they are revived.

| Item | Content |
|---|---|
| Count (§2 machine allocation) | **21 items** (Confirmed 11 / Confirmed (Revised) 8 / Pending 2) |
| 3 pillars | (A) Twin dual-persona broadcasting = method replacement / (B) Era-cycle prediction / (C) Medal scarcity |
| Common gates | opt-in, default off, human ruling required, heuristic + human judgment before data accumulation, revival from the withdrawal ledger prohibited |

**Experimental Track Common Gates**:

| # | Gate | Content |
|---|---|---|
| E-1 | **opt-in / default off** | Not exposed in the standard IA for general users; offered in stages as an advanced opt-in (V3-UIX-73) |
| E-2 | **Human ruling required** | Whether and in what form revival occurs requires a mandatory ruling by the person himself; the AI does not unilaterally reflect it into production (same gate level as V3-IND-25 / V3-AUT-44) |
| E-3 | **Cold-start discipline** | Prediction AI is not fully implemented until sufficient longitudinal data has accumulated; operated with heuristics + human judgment in the meantime (V3-AIP-84) |
| E-4 | **Revival from the withdrawal ledger prohibited** | Reviving an item covered by the withdrawal ledger (§4) requires explicit approval (restated below in §4.4) |

---

## 4.1 Twin Dual-Persona Broadcasting (Method Replacement, opt-in)

**Point at issue**: The most frequently occurring theme across all desire channels — a leading candidate the person pursued with roughly 2.5 months of persistence — but on 2026-05-31 he stated, "Having the AI carry on a natural conversation... that approach was difficult... it was wrong," thereby **withdrawing the automatic-dialogue "method" (R-3)**. Furthermore, on 2026-06-08 he decided that the persona term "Twin" would not be used in the IHL core UI. **What was rejected was the method and the name, not the underlying desire for dual-persona broadcasting itself.**

### Representative Requirements

| ID | score_v2 | Requirement (summary) | Ruling |
|---|--:|---|---|
| V3-VID-10 | 27 | Slow back-and-forth exchange between two figures — the User Twin (a character derived from the person himself) and the System Twin (the guide AI Sakura) — with the AI-side lines auto-generated from logs; other users could also create their own Twin | **Pending (human)** — the method portion is rejected under R-3, but the underlying desire persists. The form of revival requires a mandatory ruling by the person himself |
| V3-IND-25 | 4 | Separate the roles of the user-mapped AI (freely named) and the OS guide persona; ground the persona, using only real data, across the layers of language / thought / judgment / behavior / worldview. **The method is not automatic conversation generation but multi-agent + log RAG grounding**; opt-in experimental track; revival form requires human ruling | Confirmed (Revised) |
| V3-VID-11 | 12 | The automatic-dialogue method is discontinued/archived (UI/API return 410 Gone / admin-only; code moved to design/archive/). However, full manual production of videos is not adopted as a scaled-down fallback — the mass-production line (second wave) is built instead. Dual-persona broadcasting remains in the experimental track under a replacement method | Confirmed (Revised) |
| V3-VID-24 | 2 | Retain the Civilization OS main-story series (Season 1) + priority 3 episodes as content planning. Automatic script generation for the dual-persona exchange is replaced because the method was withdrawn; publication is subject to a human gate | Confirmed (Revised) |
| V3-AUT-43 | 6 | During onboarding, let the user optionally select data sources (chat logs, behavior logs, values lists, etc.) via checkboxes, giving the person transparent control over which data feeds Twin generation | Confirmed (consent UX, not subject to rejection) |
| V3-AUT-44 | 6 | The "Twin Factory" design philosophy in which anyone can have their own persona Twin + Civilization OS — expanding from a single-user OS into a platform | Confirmed (future vision; realization belongs to the experimental track / human ruling) |
| V3-UIX-65 | 6 | The persona term "Twin" (User/System Twin) is a civ-os legacy concept and is **not used at all in the IHL core UI** | Confirmed (naming scope constraint) |
| V3-UIX-73 | 2 | The evaluation UI is a shared component with role-based tag template switching. The IA is centered on the AI Fairy (standard); **the Digital Twin (behavior-model learning) is an advanced opt-in experimental track** | Confirmed (Revised) |
| V3-AIP-42 | 36 | AI features presuppose an RAG connection and do not fix a persona via prompt alone (data-driven; bypassing RAG is prohibited). Persona is likewise not characterized via prompts but formed from real logs and OK/NG differential learning | Confirmed |
| V3-OTH-19 | 12 | Persona Layer safety design: the AI is a function with no persona of its own; the persona is only performed. Decision-making, economic control, security, and personal information are outside the Persona's scope of responsibility. The person himself and the broadcast character are clearly separated | Confirmed |
| V3-SEC-53 | 9 | Individual user memory and civilization-wide shared memory are separated into two layers; personal learning data is never mixed across users | Confirmed |

### Ruling Gates (Twin Dual-Persona Broadcasting)

| Gate | Content |
|---|---|
| **Method replacement (R-3)** | What was withdrawn is "the method of having the two Twins auto-converse naturally from logs." If revived, it must be replaced with **multi-agent + log RAG grounding**; the original automatic-dialogue method is not to be revived as-is |
| **Name not used (UIX-65)** | The persona term/layer "Twin" is not brought into the IHL core user-facing UI (civ-os legacy concept) |
| **Persona = function (OTH-19/AIP-42)** | Persona is confined to a performed character; it is not granted authority over decision-making, economics, security, or PII. The person himself (the real persona) is separated from the broadcast character (a VTuber-like persona) |
| **Consent-based (AUT-43)** | Data used for Twin generation is selected by the person via checkboxes during onboarding, ensuring transparency |
| **Revival form requires the person's ruling (VID-10 pending)** | The form in which dual-persona broadcasting is revived requires a human ruling at the same gate level as V3-VID-10/AIP-74/IND-25 |

## 4.2 Era-Cycle Prediction AI (Cold Start, Experimental Track)

**Point at issue**: "Because eras go in cycles... once about 30 years of data has accumulated, the AI can predict and run it automatically" — a cultural-cycle prediction AI (a weather forecaster for culture). **The person himself acknowledges the cold-start problem: the long-term longitudinal data needed for cycle learning does not exist.** The recording and visualization layers are put into operation in Chapter 3 §3.4; **only the prediction layer is isolated into the experimental track**.

### Representative Requirements

| ID | score_v2 | Requirement (summary) | Ruling |
|---|--:|---|---|
| V3-WIK-33 | 24 | The two layers of recording → visualization are approved for the second wave; **the cycle-prediction AI remains an experimental track using heuristics + human judgment until data accumulates** | Confirmed (Revised) |
| V3-AIP-84 | 8 | Archive, compare, and restore prompt/evaluation/UI templates from era/culture data. **AI prediction of cultural cycles (e.g., 4-year cycles) is an experimental track**; operate with heuristics + human judgment until longitudinal data accumulates, and decide on full implementation only after data has accumulated | Confirmed (Revised) |
| V3-OTH-18 | 2 | The era engine (era) provides archive/compare/duplicate/restore/clustering APIs in the second wave. **Cycle prediction remains an experimental track using heuristics + human judgment until longitudinal data accumulates** | Confirmed (Revised) |
| V3-GOV-28 | 4 | Integrate the 8-axis cultural visualization dashboard into a single screen; a cultural-cycle prediction AI that extracts features and clusters past eras to predict the next era | Confirmed (the prediction portion is an experimental track) |
| V3-OBS-59 | 12 | Reproduce and search past universes via deterministic queries on snapshot_version; compare quarterly diffs in a single screen; persist Evaluator model update diffs permanently in an evolution log | Confirmed |
| V3-AIP-85 | 6 | Evaluation priority order "values > worldview > consistency > accuracy > numbers"; a de-radicalization model in which SoulProfile is referenced by all generation and philosophy nurtures the numbers | Confirmed |

### Ruling Gates (Cycle Prediction)

| Gate | Content |
|---|---|
| **Cold-start discipline** | The prediction AI is not fully implemented until data on the order of 30 years of longitudinal history has accumulated. For the time being, operate with **heuristics + human judgment**, and decide whether to fully implement after data has accumulated |
| **Separation from recording/visualization** | (a) Snapshot/rewind and (b) the dashboard are put into operation under Chapter 3 §3.4; (c) only prediction remains in the experimental track. Immaturity of prediction must not stop recording/visualization from operating |
| **No exaggeration (inherited from R-1)** | The person's own self-discipline of "delete exaggeration that doesn't actually work; only the real 3D + time line remains" is also applied to the prediction UI (autonomous evolution is not exaggerated as "already working") |

## 4.3 Medal Scarcity (Issuance Model, Human Ruling)

**Point at issue**: The platinum coin is **not currency but a civilizational medal** (not purchasable with money, no transfer/sale, inflation absolutely prohibited, fully separated from karma, issued only when a gap is filled). This **philosophy is approved**, but because **the issuance cap scheme (monthly quantity cap / fixed total supply / whether there is an annual cap / the 90% decay rate) is inconsistent across sources**, the numeric parameters remain pending until confirmed by human ruling per §5.

### Representative Requirements

| ID | score_v2 | Requirement (summary) | Ruling |
|---|--:|---|---|
| V3-MKT-38 | 64 | Approve that platinum = a civilizational medal (not purchasable with money, no transfer/sale, inflation absolutely prohibited, separated from karma, issued only when a gap is filled). **The issuance cap scheme (monthly cap / fixed total supply / annual cap, 90% decay rate) remains pending until confirmed by human ruling per §5** | Confirmed (Revised) — philosophy approved, numbers pending |
| V3-KRM-33 | 27 | Not purchasable; granted for contribution/growth/research achievement; existing holdings are never reduced; inflation is automatically controlled by AI via daily/per-user caps and a dynamic multiplier. **The monthly quantity cap and total-supply policy are integrated with V3-MKT-40 into a single consistent issuance model**; inheritance of the 90% decay for medal scarcity is subject to a separate human ruling | Confirmed (Revised) |
| V3-MKT-33 | 27 | The indulgence price follows a Fibonacci staged model (1 PT on first purchase, +1 per purchase, -1 per calendar month, floor of 1), no purchase cap; consecutive purchases self-restrain via the rising Fibonacci price (an inflation absorber) | Confirmed |
| V3-MKT-32 | 36 | A PT-consumption shop (single ledger: voting, flagging fees, and shop purchases share the same balance). Disclose inventory transparency, and **do not run promotions that stoke scarcity** | Confirmed |

### Ruling Gates (Medal Scarcity)

| Gate | Content |
|---|---|
| **Philosophy approved, numbers pending** | The philosophy of non-monetary, inflation-prohibited, karma-separated design **is approved**. The numeric parameters of the issuance cap scheme (monthly cap of 10 / fixed total supply / annual cap / 90% decay rate) **remain pending until confirmed by human ruling under §5, due to inconsistency across sources** |
| **Unify the issuance model** | Integrate V3-KRM-33 and V3-MKT-40 (double-entry bookkeeping ledger) into a **single issuance model** before implementation. Do not implement while the dual definitions remain |
| **No scarcity-stoking promotion** | Disclose transparency of inventory and issuance volume, and do not run promotions (FOMO-style) that stoke scarcity. Let the scarcity of medals arise naturally from the structure of "issued only when a gap is filled" |

---

## 4.4 Prohibition on Reviving the Withdrawal Ledger (Restated)

The experimental track handles "the restoration of desires that fell through for lack of technology," but **this restoration logic does not apply to the withdrawal ledger (Requirements Understanding Document §4)**. Items in the withdrawal ledger were withdrawn not for lack of technology but **by the user's own will (self-rejection of exaggeration, a ruling on design aesthetics)**, and **reviving them requires explicit approval**. When such a case is detected, it is fixed at Tier C and the reason is recorded in the conflict field.

Withdrawal items intersecting the 3 pillars of the experimental track:

| Code | What was withdrawn | Nature of the withdrawal | Revivability | Intersecting theme |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **R-1** | "Exaggeration that doesn't work" in the 4D Viewer, such as Semantic auto-propagation and Script auto-reaction / automatic-evolution presentation. Reduced to "only the real 3D + time line" | The person self-rejected it as "that's a lie" (2026-03-19) | **Revival prohibited** — only the real 3D + time line | §4.2 Cycle prediction / §3.4 Visualization (V3-UIX-58) |
| **R-3** | The automatic-dialogue method of "having the User Twin × System Twin auto-converse naturally from logs" | The person withdrew the method, saying "it was difficult... it was wrong" (2026-05-31). **The underlying desire is not withdrawn** | Only the method is prohibited from revival. **Permitted if replaced with multi-agent + log RAG grounding** (human ruling) | §4.1 Twin dual-persona broadcasting / §3.1 Video mass production |
| **R-9** | Expanding the Builder into "a fully integrated editing IDE beyond a UI builder" (developing the functionality itself within the Builder) | The person ruled to narrow scope on design-aesthetic grounds (the correct form is V3-UIX-08 REFRAME) | **Revival prohibited** — the REFRAME narrowing is maintained; feature development happens on GitHub | §3.2 ScreenDef generation (V3-UIX-09) |

**Discipline for judging revival**:

- Where the withdrawal is **limited to the method** (R-3) — the underlying desire is kept alive, and once the method is replaced with 2026-era technology (multi-agent/RAG grounding, etc.), it is placed in the opt-in experimental track. A ruling by the person himself is mandatory for the revival form.
- Where the withdrawal is due to **design aesthetics / self-rejection of exaggeration** (R-1/R-9) — it is outside the scope of the restoration logic. It is not revived without explicit approval.
- The correspondence between all withdrawal-ledger items (R-1 through R-9) and each applicable ID is authoritative in the Requirements Understanding Document §4; unresolved ruling issues are authoritative in §5 (Open Issues).

# Chapter 5: Non-Functional Requirements, Constraints, and the Mandatory Compatibility Layer

## 5.0 Purpose of This Chapter

This chapter is compiled as the authoritative source from the **231** entries in the final requirements registry (`ver3-要件レジストリ-v2`, 711 entries total) with **`kind=非機能要件` (Non-Functional Requirement, 57 entries)** and **`kind=制約` (Constraint, 174 entries)**. Chapters 1–4 are organized by wave (when things are built), while this chapter is an orthogonal view that cuts the same registry by type (what must be protected); **141 of the 231 entries that are Wave 1 / confirmed** (114 constraints + 27 non-functional requirements) are also reproduced in Chapter 2 (this is an overlap, not a numerical contradiction). **This chapter is the authoritative source for constraints and non-functional requirements**; identical rows in Chapters 2–4 are reproductions for reading convenience. Since ver3 redesigns the code from a zero base while **inheriting data, philosophy, and requirements from the current production system (ver3-live)** (per the DIFF-C-05 decision), the constraints in this chapter function as invariant conditions that the new implementation must not violate (excluding rows tagged pending/rejected).

The classification axis prioritizes non-functional themes that recur across desire themes (availability / cost / security / reproducibility / append-only layer-separation rules / authentication boundaries / PII), with the remainder grouped into 10 buckets by domain and by development process. Bucket assignment followed a deterministic rule (same input → same classification) that prioritized cross-cutting themes (PII → append-only → reproducibility → availability) first, then the registry's `category` field.

The `要件文` (Requirement Text) column for each row uses the full `statement` field from the registry verbatim as the authoritative source, without summarization. `trust` (extraction confidence), `人確` (human_confirm = whether a human-confirmation gate is required), `wave` (implementation wave), and `ver2` (`status_in_ver2` = implementation status in the current production system) are shown alongside it.

> **How to read this**: Append-only (§5.2.1, 42 entries) and Development Process/AI Operations Gates (§5.2.8, 43 entries) together account for about 37% of the total. The former is the immutable rule set for the Truth layer (the basis for the §5.3 ADR), and the latter is development discipline such as the critic gate that ensures we never claim a non-working feature "works" — both are foundational conditions for the ver3 implementation.
